Title: Maris Amri et Slibae De patriarchis Nestorianorum commentaria (archive.org catalogue title). Pars altera: Amri et Slibae textus (file line 6475) and Amri et Slibae textus, versio Latina (file lines 14959 to 14961), ex codicibus Vaticanis edidit ac Latine reddidit Henricus Gismondi S.J.
Creator: Amr ibn Matta of Tirhan (fourteenth century) and Sliba ibn Yuhanna of Mosul, whose two recensions Gismondi prints together; Enrico (Henricus) Gismondi S.J. (ed. and Latin trans.)
Publisher: Romae: excudebat F. de Luigi (Arabic text, file line 6479) and C. de Luigi (Latin version, file line 14965); printed by the Tipografia della Casa Editrice Italiana, Roma 1896 and 1897 (file lines 6454 and 14928)
Publication Date: 1896 (Arabic text; Gismondi's preface dated Romae Idibus Aprilis MDCCCXCVI at file line 6370) and 1897 (Latin version; MDCCCXCVII at file line 14967)
Rights: Public Domain (pd-us-by-date: published 1896-1897); the item record carries no rights field
Language: lat (Latin only recovered: a character count found no Arabic script, so the Arabic text part, file lines 1 to about 6475, is not usable from this OCR. As in the companion pars prior file, both parts are scanned in reverse page order, so the Latin version's title page is at 14959 near the end and its text runs backwards.)
Source: archive.org item marisamrietslib3v4mari (PIMS - University of Toronto; catalogue volume 3-4), full text at https://archive.org/download/marisamrietslib3v4mari/marisamrietslib3v4mari_djvu.txt. Fetched directly 2026-10-17 by the Shared-Events source-research thread (Era 2 worker C); the byte count of the downloaded file (493,336) equals the size archive.org lists for the djvu.txt.
Provenance note: Extracted unaltered: the item's own djvu.txt is reproduced whole after this header; nothing was stripped, normalized or corrected, and the reversed page order is left as scanned. Rights basis: Rome printings of 1896 and 1897 are out of US copyright under this project's pd-us-by-date rule.
Content note: Amr and Sliba's lives of the patriarchs of the East in Gismondi's Latin (read the Latin pages in descending file order): Dadisho's election, his withdrawal to the monastery of the Ark and his death at file lines 14072 to 14097; Yazdgard's death in his twenty-second year at 14166; Isaac's death in Yazdgard's twelfth year, A.Gr. 728, and burial at al-Mada'in after eleven years at 14185 to 14188; Ahai from 14189; Yahballaha at 14223. Fourteenth-century Church of the East memory witnesses; never evidence of 410 or 424 on their own. The Arabic of this same part is vendored from a Beth Mardutho scan that does recover it.

parisiensi  (Bibliothecae  national.)  CXC  supplere  licuit,  brevi,  Deo 
favente,  me  editurum  confido,  tunc  forte  prolaturus  si  quid  triam 
lucubrationum  penitior  inspectio  suggesserit  quod  redigat  ad  verum 
de  Amro  ac  Sliba  dubitationem,   uter  opus  alterius  fecerit  suum. 

Gratias  habeo  quam  plurimas  cl.  Ignatio  Guidi  qui  summa  qua 
praestat  humanitate  perutili  me  iuvit  opera  in  hac  paranda  editione.

Utriusque  textus  coUatio  ideni  prorsus  opus  prodit,  quuiii   res 

eaedeni  iisdem  fere  continenter  vocabulis  expressae  in  utroque  de- 

prehendantur,  praeter  pauca  quae  a  Sliba  addita  dicenda  sunt,  nisi 

quis  verius  duxerit  Amrum  quae  in  Slibae  opere  superflua  videban- 

tur  resecuisse.  Hinc  licuit  utramque  uno  cbnspectu  elucubrationem 

exhibere.    Slibae  nempe  textum  protuli,  quae  ei  prae  Amro  pro- 

pria  sunl  uncinis  inclusa  repraesentans,  opus  tamen  Amro  inscripsi 

auctori  scilicet  primo,  ut  fert  opinio:  cuius  propria  aliquot,  a  quibus 

discrepal  Sliba,  seorsim  collecta  in  appendice  recensui.  A  differen- 

tiis  aliis  indicandis  abstinui  quae  in  usu  synonvinorum  consistunt, 

vel  in  diverso  ordine  recensendi   quae   apud   utrumque   auctorem 

eadem  sunt,  ut  alicuius  Primatis  anni  episcopatus  aut  vitae,   dies 

obitus,  locus  sepulturae  et   similia:   vocalia   signa   subinde  adieci 

ad  sensus  perspicuitatem.  Dictionis  soloecismos,  quorum  complures 

ad  vulgare  eius  temporis  eloquiura  scriptionemque  pertinent,  ut  in 

textu  sunt,  retinui,  nisi  nimiae  quandoque  offensioni  lectori  essent, 

ut  quae   emendavi  pag.   1,   lin.  1:  (Jj>^\  JL_«.>  ^[jil ,  et  lin.  3: 

JiSt'  Luj  ^fJac  Jk^   aliaque   huiusmodi   haud   multa   quae    Ubra- 

rio  potius  indocto  quam  scriptori  nostro  sat  erudito  imputanda 

videntur.

^ommentaria  quae  de  Patriarchis  Nestorianorum  Mares  filius 
Salomonis  suo  libro  Turris  intexuit  Amrum  Matthaei  tirhanen- 
sem  retractasse,  nec  non  illud  Amri  opus  Slibam  lohannis  mossu- 
lensem  ei  coaevum  paulo  aliter  digessisse,  recepere  qui  rei  eccle- 
siasticae  orientalium  historiara  vestigaverunt.  Opus  quod  Amro 
tribuitur  exhibet  vaticanus  codex  inter  arabicos  CX  pervetustus, 
foliis  perperam  ac  temere  assutis,  qui  utpote  emendationibus  notu- 
lisque  eodem  caiamo  exaratis  passim  conspersus,  autographi  potius 
quam  transcripti  exemplaris  speciem  refert.  Uberiorem  Slibae  recen- 
sionem  codex  alius  continet,  iam  pridem  ad  bibliothecara  Neophyto- 
rum  s.  Mariae  ad  Montes  de  Ui'be  pertinens,  aliquot  abhinc  annis 
inter  vatic.  arab.  (Neoph.XLI)  adnumeratus(').  Hoc  is  incipit  initio  :

Borgiano  Ser.  K.  W,  vol.  14 :  id  usui  mihi  fuit  ad  scripta  quandoque  incerta 
rite  legenda.

Columnis  VII,  VIII,  IX  n.)tantur:  quotus  dies  mensis  sit  in  singulis  annis 
occuri-onlibus  domiiiica  paschalis,  liltera  dominicalis,  et  epacta:  (adhibuimus 
opus  P.  Escofher  S.  I.:  Calandrier  Perpetuel,  Paris  1880). 

Columna  X  notatur  plaeniluniuin  cyclicum  supputatum  iuxta  ordinem 
epactarum  (opus  cit.  p.  349).  Columna  XI  nostras  litteras  dominicales  expressas 
exhibet  per  numeros  juxta  rationem  methodi  nostri  auctoris.  ut  jiaulo  su- 
perius  notalum  est. 

Colanina  V  comparanda  est  cum  X:  maxima,  quae  sic  apparet,  aoqualitas 
numerorum  plenilunii  (plus  quam  septuagies)  multum  augetur  (plus  quam 
centies),  si  animadvortitur  numeros  asterisco  notatos  orratos  esse  non  apud 
nostrum  auctorem  sed  ex  mendosa  transcriptione:  facile  enim  in  scriptura 
syriaoa  nesloriana  legenti  et  transcrihcnti  confundere  accidit  soquenles  lilteras: 
fie  =  5  cum  uau  =  G,  kaf  —  20  cum  belh  =  2,  Idmadh  =  30  cum 
dlaf  =  1,  itein  jodh  =  10  onfundere  cum  tractu  inchoante  litteram  se- 
quentem,  aut  sequonli  forte  iiau  cuniungere  legendo  he.  Ad  id  autem  quod  modo 
dictuni  est  de  hunadli  facile  cum  dlaf  permutabih  addatur  animadversio 
posse  plaenilunium  in  eodem  mense  bis  accidere  nempe  30"  et  1"  die,  hinc 
solet  scribi  30,  1:  alia  idcirco,  hoc  in  casu.  sit  interdum  ratio  assumenda  ad 
numeri  differentiam  oxplicandam. 

Porro  inter  plonihinium  cyclicum  deductum  iuxla  tabulas  epactales,  ac 
iuxta  cyclum  19  annoruin,  difierentia  est  diei  unius,  nec  enim  hic  alter 
supputationem  ad  unguera  exactam  admittit,  hinc  etiam  in  reformatione  gre- 
goriana  fere  relictus  est.

nicales),  unde  noscitur  Dominica  Resurrectlonis  quotus  dies  sit  mensis,  Jdartii 
quidem.  ut  plurimum,  si  illud  dieni  21  excedit,  Aprilis  si  secus.

schaiia  unius  cycii  noscunlur  quo  anni  die  accidant,  id  eliam  iKilum  erit  de
